# Implementation status

The single place to see what is **done**, **in progress**, **blocked** and **next**. Update it in the same commit as the work. Plans stay the contract (`plans/`); this file only tracks execution. History (old Resume-here blocks, per-task evidence tables, findings, full session log) lives in [`docs/status-archive.md`](docs/status-archive.md); keep this file short.

Legend: `done` (acceptance checks run and passed) · `doing` · `blocked (<who>)` · `todo` · `partial` (see note).

**Recorded at the owner's request 2026-10-03** (start at the next session; resume phrase "tiếp tục theo STATUS.md"). Tiers are proposed; the orchestrator raises them per `~/.omp/agent/RULES.md`. Web UI (plan 10) stays in its own session. The Session 4 plan is in the archive.

**Step 0: fix the CI `integration` hang on `develop` first.** #119 was merged while CI run `37131498756` was still running: build-test, contract and migrations green; `integration` hung (`GenerateStoryQaTests.Generate_RequestBody_IsIgnored_TriggerAlwaysUser` waited 6 min on `POST /api/v1/episodes/{id}/story/generate`, then `TaskCanceledException`; local passes) and was cancelled. Find the root cause with evidence (`pg_stat_activity` / `pg_locks` during the hang, or a partial CI log of a rerun cancelled at ~12 min), fix it on a branch off `develop` (`fix/ci-generate-story-hang`), full gate, PR, CI 4/4, then the owner merges. Then remove worktrees `09.2.5-T2`, `02.3.4-T1`, `02.2.2-T2`, `02.5.1-T1`, `s4b-combined` and their local + remote branches.

**Not in P:** 09.3.2-T2 per-request suspension is phase S (`S · M2`); 09.3.4 QA-2 stays accepted until the S go decision. 09.6.2-T1's dependency 05.1.2-T1 is merged (#197).

**Working method (the same as Session 4; the owner merges):** code the lanes in parallel, one worktree per task; each task closes its own tier gates (QA agent first, then reviews, fixes, a delta check, a full gate at its final tip); then all tasks of the wave go into **one combined PR** (`feature/s5-wave-combined`), merged in dependency order, with a full gate on the combined tip, an integration review and CI 4/4 before the owner merges it.

**Rules for every agent in this plan:**
1. One worktree + branch per task: `../nts-wt/<task-id>`, branch `feature/<task-id>-s5`, off current `develop`. No stacked branches on unmerged work.
2. At most 8 subagents at a time. Code in parallel; **gates run one at a time** (`/Volumes/Project/nts-wt/gate.sh <worktree>`), because all worktrees share the local Postgres template `nts_template` (parallel runs fail with 23505). Exception: the remote gate (`/Volumes/Project/nts-wt/gate-remote.sh <worktree>`, Azure VM with its own Postgres and lock) may run in parallel with one local gate; it is the second backend lane and needs the same owner approval (`.omp/coordinator/PROJECT.md` §Gate). Capture the gate exit code before pushing; remote exit code 3 means the VM was unreachable and is not a gate result.
3. Migrations: at most 2 tasks with a migration waiting for gate/merge at once. Merge them in order; the later task **regenerates** its migration on the merged `develop` snapshot (`dotnet ef migrations add`), never hand-edits `AppDbContextModelSnapshot`, moves `migrations[^1]` ownership to its own `*MigrationTests` and adds a `SeedPrevious` row (`docs/dev-setup.md`).
4. Conflicts in `Program.cs` (`Map*` lines) and `AppDbContext.cs` (`DbSet` lines) are additive: keep both sides; each `DbSet` keeps its own `///` summary.
5. Every implementer follows the "Implementer brief" (precedent footprint, Acceptance self-check, one `BUILD_ONLY=1` gate) and `docs/dev-setup.md` §Host boot contexts for start-up code / required keys. Each task closes its tier gates (QA agent first, then reviews) before its PR. After any merge, check `git merge-base --is-ancestor <branch-tip> origin/develop`.
6. Local services: start `weed` and `mailpit` only through `tools/dev/up.sh` (loopback flags), never `brew services`.
7. Update this table in the task's own branch (STATUS row + Session log); after the combined PR merges, remove every worktree and the local + remote branches.
8. Known open items, not blockers: phase-P residuals (platform-wide budget without a per-user share until 12.1.2; source routes without a rate-limit policy; one retried timed-out OpenAI attempt may be billed but unmetered; 13.3 erasure must delete `source_creations`), CY-01…CY-30 fix waves W1–W4 (`cyber-issue.md`), SEC-01 synthetic seed (`09.3.1-T2` before registration opens).

## Plan 10 hand-overs (from Stage A, 10 CR-45)

Remove each line when its owner closes it. The Session 6 plan is archived (`docs/status-archive.md` §Resume-here history).
- **Backend (.NET session, 09.x):** `POST /api/v1/auth/register` and `GET /health/live` carry the dotted tag `NightTales.Api`, which breaks `ng-openapi-gen`, so `web/api-gen.config.json` `excludeTags` drops them (no web client for register yet). Fix with `WithTags(...)`, then remove the exclusion. Also: no operation has an `operationId`, and the `ProblemDetails` schema has no `errors` field (field-level validation errors cannot be typed in the web client).
- **11.1.1-T1 / 11.5.1-T1:** the `web` container runs on a read-only root filesystem only with `--tmpfs /tmp` and `--tmpfs /etc/nginx/conf.d:uid=101,gid=101,mode=0755` (as in `web/tools/web/container-smoke.sh`). **11.5.1-T1** `e2e.yml` must also exclude or scrub `*.md`/`*.png` from auth-spec results before upload (they can hold the TOTP manual key). Both are in the task Notes.
- **10.4.1-T1 lane (from 10.4.2-T1):** dashboard i18n `{{title}}` keys are unconfirmed; the dashboard owner investigates and closes that path; user titles must not be Transloco params. (The episode-list path, `episode-filters.component.html`, was fixed by 10.5.2-T1, Session 25.)

The tests that assert the URL path `/dashboard` (returnUrl fallback, sign-in) need a matching route: 10.4.1-T1 added `/dashboard` (merged #149, `070b8e6`, Session 20). Observed on the #149 tip `254113d` (tree identical to `070b8e6`), stubbed: `auth-login`/`errors`/`session` give 53 passed, 3 skipped, 6 failed (before #149: 17 `/dashboard`-path failures). The 6 are `session.spec.ts` L153/L171/L220 (vi + en): their `signIn` helper asserts the path `/dashboard`, but the stubbed hub ticket answers 401 `session_revoked` ~30 ms after login, so the session ends before the router commits the lazy `/dashboard` route (probe: no `/dashboard` navigation, no episode request). Spec-side race, not a dashboard defect; **10.3.3-T3** (or the 10.3.3-T1 owner) drops the `/dashboard` assertion from `signIn` in those three tests. **11.5.1-T1** `e2e.yml` runs them; set `NTS_E2E_STACK=1` for the real-stack blocks in `auth-login.spec.ts`/`auth-reset.spec.ts`.
- **Plan 11 deploy (from 10.2.4-T1, 10 CR-48; no task file edited):** the production load balancer/CDN must not log the `/hubs/episodes` query string (single-use 60 s ticket; the nginx guards cover dev/compose only). (`web/e2e/realtime.spec.ts` was written by 10.5.2-T1, #160, stubbed; its real-stack run is the 11.5.1-T1 row in §Open tasks.)
- **10.2.6-T1 (Session 13):** the not-found `**` route is **not** shipped: no approved not-found design or copy keys exist, and a `**` → `''` redirect would loop through `authGuard` → `/login` until 10.3.3-T1 adds `/login` (owner 2026-10-05). It needs a design task (T0) and an owner; when added it stays the LAST `app.routes.ts` entry and must not bypass the shell guards. `web/e2e/shell.spec.ts` authenticates with `page.route` mocks (refresh, `/me`, hub ticket) because `/login` does not exist yet; 10.3.3-T1 may switch it to `loginAsOwner`. No route set `data.titleKey` before 10.4.1-T1; `/dashboard` (merged #149) sets `shell.title.dashboard`, so the document-title-on-locale-change e2e assertion can now target it (not added by 10.4.1-T1).
- **Backend (09.x, owner decision 2026-10-05, from 10.3.3-T3):** `POST /api/v1/auth/login` answers 401 `mfa_enrolment_required` **without** the enrolment-only token (`ITokenIssuer.IssueEnrolmentToken` has no production caller; `RefreshTokenTests.Login_OwnerWithoutMfa_ReturnsMfaEnrolmentRequired_WithoutTokens` pins "no enrolment token on the wire, WT22/WT39"), contradicting `09.10.4-T1` Steps 5 and the web task. Until a backend follow-up issues it in the 401 body (`token`, read by the web `LoginOutcome` `enrolment`), a fresh owner cannot enrol through the UI and the `NTS_E2E_STACK=1` blocks of `web/e2e/auth-{mfa,enrolment,reauth}.spec.ts` fail by design (10.3.3-T3 task §Notes).

## Deferred security gates

**Deferred security gates (security review 2026-09-29, must close before any feature endpoint/command/query lands):** Closed 2026-09-30: `AuthorizationBehavior` (09.1.3-T2, #54) is deny-by-default (anonymous → `unauthorized`, insufficient role → `not_found`, system actor passes only `User`); the tenant `HasQueryFilter` (09.2.3-T1, #52) covers all 8 `IUserOwned` entities on both contexts; anonymous sees no rows. Closed 2026-10-01: production `Program.cs` registers ES256 JwtBearer (09.3.2-T1, #60), so feature endpoints may merge; the series routes (09.4.1-T1) are its first consumer.

## Open findings

Closed findings and their full context: `docs/status-archive.md` §Findings during implementation.

- **S51-1** (10.5.8-T1, S51, 2026-10-17) — `[OPEN]` owner: TL / an `ERROR_CODES` change. The 02.7 §7 job-outcome code `find_sources_generation_failed` cannot be catalogued in `web/src/assets/i18n/errors/{en,vi}.json`: the orphan rule in `core/errors/error-code.spec.ts` forbids a leaf for a code that is not in `ERROR_CODES`, and job-outcome codes are not listed there. The copy ships at `episodes.sources.error.find_sources_generation_failed` (episodes scope, 02.7 §6 text verbatim). Catalogue it properly once `ERROR_CODES` (or the orphan rule) allows job-outcome codes. The task text's path (`core/{vi,en}.json`) is stale: the errors scope lives at `web/src/assets/i18n/errors/`. (from 10.5.8-T1, S51)
- **S51-2** (10.5.8-T1, S51, 2026-10-17) — `[OPEN]` owner: 02.7.2-T3 (ref S49-1). The `source_finder` flag must stay off until 02.7.2-T3 ships the Worker consumer of `SourceFindRequested`: with the flag on, a real run never finishes (the run stays `queued` until `StaleGenerationSweeperJob` fails it, and each search blocks the episode with 409 `find_sources_in_progress`), so the panel's running state never resolves; the e2e stubs the API. (from 10.5.8-T1, S51, ref S49-1)
- **S59-1** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: TL (backend decision, owner decision needed). "Edit scenes" cannot be offered from a `failed` episode with today's server: `EpisodeNextActions.cs:118` gives `failed` only `retry_render` + `cancel`, and `ReopenStoryCommandHandler` accepts only `story_approved`. The page offers the button only when `reopen_story` is in `nextActions` and `approval.canReopen()` holds, so for `image_moderated` the owner sees the scene list and the hint but no button (task failure-handling row "button absent"). Fix needs a backend change (offer `reopen_story` and accept `failed → story_review`) or a decision that the owner retries instead.
- **S59-2** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: 11.5.1-T1. The e2e (`web/e2e/render-timeline.spec.ts`, 18 tests = 9 × vi/en) runs against `page.route` stubs and a hub stub, not the compose stack with mock-mpt (`MOCK_RENDER_SECONDS`, `MOCK_FAIL_MODE`, `MOCK_IMAGE_REPORT`); the real-stack acceptance run is open (this lane runs no compose stack).
- **S59-3** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: 10.6.1-T2. The failure panel's retry button is rendered disabled: the page filters `retry_render` out of `actions` (`StoryReviewPage.renderActions`) and the `(retry)` output is unbound. 10.6.1-T2 binds it, stops the filter, and adds make-video and cancel to the render branch.
- **S59-4** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: 10.2.4-T1 / WB2. (a) `RenderProgress` carries no job id (`{stage, percent}`), so a leftover progress entry of a previous job of the same episode (re-make path, 10.6.2-T1) is merged into a fresh job: it moves the percent and, for a `queued` detail, now also promotes the step to Running, until the detail refetch (`render-timeline.store.ts`, comment at the `job` computed). (b) `queuedSince` is stamped only while the job is observed as `queued`; a job first seen already promoted by a live stage, or `submitted`, without `submittedAt` shows 0:00 until the next detail read.
- **S59-5** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: 03.4.2-T1 (backend DTO) / TL. `RenderJobSummaryDto` has no `images` field (neither the `web/openapi.json` pin nor the `develop` DTO), so the skipped-scene list and the `image_moderated` guidance never show with a real server; the mapper reads `images` defensively and the specs use a contract-true fake (task Notes O-2). `web/openapi.json` was not re-pinned: no render field is missing except `images`.
- **S59-6** (10.6.1-T1, S59, 2026-10-18) — `[OPEN]` owner: TL to assign (accepted lows, none blocks an Acceptance check). C3 `RenderFailure.detailCode` and `imageDetailCode` are produced but no template reads them; C5 `clampPercent` lives in `application/` and is duplicated in the timeline component (layering); C6 two i18n key conventions (`errors.<code>` qualified, `render.*` re-prefixed by two components); C9 the screen-reader elapsed summary is frozen per stage and the visible clock is `aria-hidden`; C11 `render.images.skippedBanner` has no consumer until 10.6.2-T1; PS-4 a `rendering` episode with `renderJob: null` shows the skeleton forever if the single refetch fails (recovers on the next `EpisodeUpdated`); PS-5 the skipped-scene list is not capped client-side (server bounds it); QA-O1 a lower live percent lowers the bar (not monotone by construction); QA-O2 a non-`failed` job carrying a leftover `errorCode` opens the failure panel; an episode `failed` whose job maps to `cancelled` (or an unknown wire status) shows three `todo` steps and no failure panel.
- **FLAKE-S36** (02.5.5-T1 full gate, 2026-10-15) — `[OPEN]` `NightTales.Fetcher.UnitTests.FetcherLoopTests.Loop_FetchSource_PastT105Bound_PostsNothing` failed once under parallel gate load (`Wait.UntilAsync` timed out, `Sequence contains no elements`, `FetcherLoopTests.cs:222`) on a tree that does not touch the Fetcher; it passes alone and in the second full run (`GATE_RC=0`). Same pattern as `EndToEnd_SourceDeletedMidFetch`. Owner: TL (timing-bound triage; quarantine only if it recurs).
- **PF1-S39** (05.1.2-T1 full gate, 2026-10-15) — `[OPEN]` `NightTales.Api.IntegrationTests.Migrations.AddFactSheetsMigrationTests.Pf1Index_ServesTheNewestRunOfOneEpisode` fails deterministically when it runs with the other `Migrations` tests (`TEST_FILTER=FullyQualifiedName~Migrations`: fails; alone: passes): the `EXPLAIN` shows `Limit → Sort` instead of `ix_generation_runs_episode_created` (`AddFactSheetsMigrationTests.cs:281`). Reproduced on pristine `develop` `489d26a4` (detached worktree), so it is not caused by 05.1.2-T1 (no DB, no migration). Two full runs on the merged tree gave the same single failure. Owner: TL; next migration-lane task (02.6.3-T1 S37 or later) or a dedicated test fix (coordinator 2026-10-15: does not block #197). Keep `[OPEN]`.
